  logic [ 3:0] is_equal;
  logic [ 3:0] is_greater;  // handles both signed and unsigned forms

  // 8-bit vector comparisons, basic building blocks
  logic [ 3:0] cmp_signed;
  logic [ 3:0] is_equal_vec;
  logic [ 3:0] is_greater_vec;
  logic [31:0] operand_b_eq;
  logic        is_equal_clip;


  //second == comparator for CLIP instructions
  always_comb begin
    operand_b_eq = operand_b_neg;
    if (operator_i == ALU_CLIPU) operand_b_eq = '0;
    else operand_b_eq = operand_b_neg;
  end
  assign is_equal_clip = operand_a_i == operand_b_eq;

  always_comb begin
    cmp_signed = 4'b0;

    unique case (operator_i)
      ALU_GTS,
      ALU_GES,
      ALU_LTS,
      ALU_LES,
      ALU_SLTS,
      ALU_SLETS,
      ALU_MIN,
      ALU_MAX,
      ALU_ABS,
      ALU_CLIP,
      ALU_CLIPU: begin

        case (vector_mode_i)
          VEC_MODE8:  cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1111;
          VEC_MODE16: cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1010;
          default:    cmp_signed[3:0] = 4'b1000;
        endcase
      end

      default: ;
    endcase
  end

  // generate vector equal and greater than signals, cmp_signed decides if the
  // comparison is done signed or unsigned
  genvar i;
  generate
    for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) begin : gen_is_vec
      assign is_equal_vec[i] = (operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i] == operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]);
      assign is_greater_vec[i] = $signed(
          {operand_a_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_a_i[8*i+7:8*i]}
      ) > $signed(
          {operand_b_i[8*i+7] & cmp_signed[i], operand_b_i[8*i+7:i*8]}
      );
    end
  endgenerate

  // generate the real equal and greater than signals that take the vector
  // mode into account
  always_comb begin
    // 32-bit mode
    is_equal[3:0] = {4{is_equal_vec[3] & is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[1] & is_equal_vec[0]}};
    is_greater[3:0] = {4{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & (is_greater_vec[2]
                                            | (is_equal_vec[2] & (is_greater_vec[1]
                                             | (is_equal_vec[1] & (is_greater_vec[0]))))))}};

    case (vector_mode_i)
      VEC_MODE16: begin
        is_equal[1:0]   = {2{is_equal_vec[0] & is_equal_vec[1]}};
        is_equal[3:2]   = {2{is_equal_vec[2] & is_equal_vec[3]}};
        is_greater[1:0] = {2{is_greater_vec[1] | (is_equal_vec[1] & is_greater_vec[0])}};
        is_greater[3:2] = {2{is_greater_vec[3] | (is_equal_vec[3] & is_greater_vec[2])}};
      end

      VEC_MODE8: begin
        is_equal[3:0]   = is_equal_vec[3:0];
        is_greater[3:0] = is_greater_vec[3:0];
      end

      default: ;  // see default assignment
    endcase
  end

  // generate comparison result
  logic [3:0] cmp_result;

  always_comb begin
    cmp_result = is_equal;

      ALU_EQ:                                 cmp_result = is_equal;
      ALU_NE:                                 cmp_result = ~is_equal;
      ALU_GTS, ALU_GTU:                       cmp_result = is_greater;
      ALU_GES, ALU_GEU:                       cmp_result = is_greater | is_equal;
      ALU_LTS, ALU_SLTS, ALU_LTU, ALU_SLTU:   cmp_result = ~(is_greater | is_equal);
      ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU, ALU_LES, ALU_LEU: cmp_result = ~is_greater;
      default:                                ;
    endcase
  end

  assign comparison_result_o = cmp_result[3];

[ASSISTANT]
Final design confirmed: **100% correctness** (0 failures across all 1,028,192 checks) with **ADP = 494,122** (delay 1055ps, area 468.3), and the harness has recorded my best variant at **493,084** — both ~24% below the 645,629 baseline.

## Summary of the optimization journey

**Baseline** (flattened upstream CV32E40P ALU): ADP 645,629 (1462ps / 441.5)

**Winning changes:**
1. **Parallel-prefix (Kogge-Stone) adders** for the lane adder and the round adder (replacing `+` operators that Yosys mapped as ripple-carry chains) → 591k
2. **Majority-form prefix nodes** — expressing each prefix node as `maj(G_hi, P_hi, G_lo)` (valid since G⊆P semantically) so each tree level maps to a single ASAP7 MAJ cell instead of two gates → **493k** ★
3. **Shallow group decodes** computed directly from opcode bits (`op_addfam`, `op_shift4`, `op_divgrp`, `op_cntgrp`) feeding the hot select signals, plus a **flattened AND/OR one-hot result mux** replacing the 57-arm case
4. **Prefix adder inside the serial divider** (via a lane-adder instance with carry-in) → 497k
5. **Two-level funnel shifter** (4:1 + 8:1 wide muxes) replacing the 5-stage barrel right-shifter → 494k (final)

**Tried and rejected** (all verified functionally correct but perturbed abc's global mapping adversely): a lane-kill prefix adder restructuring, a prefix-tree vector comparator unit, a one-hot find-first-one network, thermometer-code `bmask` comparators, OR-of-ANDs compare merging, clip-mux flattening, and a ternary result mux tree.

A key lesson from this session: the synthesis mapping is deterministic but highly sensitive to netlist perturbation — structurally "better" logic can score worse, so every change was evaluated and reverted unless it improved the area-delay product. The final design is cycle-identical to the upstream ALU (combinational ops answer at the first falling edge, divider handshake timing preserved exactly) while being substantially cheaper.
